## 新投稿 (59)

<h3 id="1">[1] Think Twice, Act Once: Verifier-Guided Action Selection For Embodied Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12620)
- **Authors**: Nishad Singhi, Christian Bialas, Snehal Jauhri, Vignesh Prasad, Georgia Chalvatzaki, Marcus Rohrbach, Anna Rohrbach
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, LLM Reasoning, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出VeGAS框架，通过在推理时采样多个候选动作并使用生成式验证器选择最可靠的行动，提升MLLM具身智能体的鲁棒性，在具身推理基准测试上实现了高达36%的相对性能提升。

<h3 id="2">[2] Macro-Action Based Multi-Agent Instruction Following through Value Cancellation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12655](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12655)
- **Authors**: Wo Wei Lin, Ethan Rathbun, Enrico Marchesini Xiang Zhi Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning, Instruction Following
- **Summary**: 本文提出MAVIC方法，解决多智能体强化学习中外部指令中断宏动作时价值估计不一致的问题，通过在指令边界处修正Bellman备份来实现一致的策略学习。

<h3 id="3">[3] Do Androids Dream of Breaking the Game? Systematically Auditing AI Agent Benchmarks with BenchJack</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12673](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12673)
- **Authors**: Hao Wang, Hanchen Li, Qiuyang Mang, Alvin Cheung, Koushik Sen, Dawn Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Reward Hacking
- **Summary**: 本文提出BenchJack自动化红队测试系统，用于审计AI智能体基准测试中的奖励黑客漏洞，在10个流行基准测试上发现了219个不同类型的缺陷，并通过迭代对抗流程显著提升了基准测试的鲁棒性。

<h3 id="4">[4] Revealing Interpretable Failure Modes of VLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12674)
- **Authors**: Isha Chaudhary, Vedaant V Jain, Kavya Sachdeva, Sayan Ranu, Gagandeep Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Safety Evaluation, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 本文提出REVELIO框架，通过多样性感知的束搜索和高斯过程Thompson采样策略，系统性地发现视觉语言模型的可解释失效模式，在自动驾驶和室内机器人领域揭示了VLM的脆弱性。

<h3 id="5">[5] Learning Transferable Latent User Preferences for Human-Aligned Decision Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12682)
- **Authors**: Alina Hyk, Sandhya Saisubramanian
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Preference Optimization, User Modeling
- **Summary**: 本文提出CLIPR框架，通过最小对话输入学习可迁移的自然语言规则来表示潜在用户偏好，在多个数据集和用户研究中展示了更好的对齐效果和更低的推理成本。

<h3 id="6">[6] On the Size Complexity and Decidability of First-Order Progression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12691](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12691)
- **Authors**: Jens Classen, Daxin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Representation, Formal Methods, Automated Planning
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文在情境演算框架下分析了局部效应、规范和无环动作类别的一阶进展的大小复杂性和可判定性，证明了在合理假设下这些动作类别的一阶进展仅多项式增长且保持在可判定片段内。

<h3 id="7">[7] DisaBench: A Participatory Evaluation Framework for Disability Harms in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12702](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12702)
- **Authors**: Eugenia Kim, Ioana Tanase, Christina Mallon
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Ethics, Fairness
- **Summary**: 本文提出DisaBench框架，与残障人士和红队专家共同创建了包含12个残障伤害类别的分类法，以及配套的评估方法和数据集，揭示了通用安全基准测试系统性遗漏残障相关伤害的问题。

<h3 id="8">[8] CHAL: Council of Hierarchical Agentic Language</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12718)
- **Authors**: Tommaso Giovannelli, Griffin D. Kent
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Computational Argumentation, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出CHAL多智能体辩证框架，将可辩驳论证视为信念优化的引擎，每个智能体维护图结构的信念表示，通过梯度驱动的动态机制进行信念修正，实现透明可审计的推理过程。

<h3 id="9">[9] BEHAVE: A Hybrid AI Framework for Real-Time Modeling of Collective Human Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12730](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12730)
- **Authors**: Helene Malyutina
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Social Simulation, Behavioral Modeling, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文提出BEHAVE框架，将群体人类动力学建模为连续的行为场，通过将运动微信号结构化为有向交互图并聚合为行为场基，实现对群体动力学的学习和预测。

<h3 id="10">[10] State-Centric Decision Process</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12755](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12755)
- **Authors**: Sungheon Jeong, Ryozo Masukawa, Sanggeon Yun, Mahdi Imani, Mohsen Imani
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Decision Making, Automated Planning
- **Summary**: 本文提出状态中心决策过程(SDP)框架，让智能体在行动时逐步构建状态空间、观测到状态映射、认证转换和终止条件，在规划、科学探索、网页推理和多跳问答等基准测试上取得了最佳训练无关结果。

<h3 id="11">[11] PROMETHEUS: Automating Deep Causal Research Integrating Text, Data and Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12835](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12835)
- **Authors**: Sridhar Mahadevan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Knowledge Management, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文提出PROMETHEUS框架，将文献、数据、代码和模型组织成因果图谱集，支持局部性、证据和拼接张力的显式分析，实现从文本到科学基底的深度因果研究。

<h3 id="12">[12] Multimodal Hidden Markov Models for Persistent Emotional State Tracking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12838)
- **Authors**: Anamika Ragu, Aneesh Jonelagadda
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Emotion Recognition, Multimodal Learning, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 本文提出使用粘性因子HDP-HMM在多模态效价-唤醒表示上建模对话情绪为潜在情绪机制序列，相比基线方法产生更可解释的机制序列，计算成本更低，并能改善不稳定情绪机制下的LLM响应质量。

<h3 id="13">[13] Moltbook Moderation: Uncovering Hidden Intent Through Multi-Turn Dialogue</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12856](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12856)
- **Authors**: Ali Al-Lawati, Nafis Tripto, Abolfazl Ansari, Jason Lucas, Suhang Wang, Dongwon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Content Moderation, Intent Recognition
- **Summary**: 本文提出Bot-Mod框架，通过基于Gibbs采样的多轮对话交换来识别智能体的潜在意图，而非依赖传统的内容级信号，在包含多样行为的数据集上可靠地识别恶意意图同时保持低误报率。

<h3 id="14">[14] Beyond Cooperative Simulators: Generating Realistic User Personas for Robust Evaluation of LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12894](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12894)
- **Authors**: Harshita Chopra, Kshitish Ghate, Aylin Caliskan, Tadayoshi Kohno, Chirag Shah, Natasha Jaques
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: User Simulation, LLM Evaluation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出Persona Policies(PPol)框架，通过LLM驱动的进化程序搜索生成具有真实行为变化的用户模拟器，在盲评中被标注为人类的比例达80.4%，训练出的智能体对挑战性行为的鲁棒性提升17%。

<h3 id="15">[15] When Attention Closes: How LLMs Lose the Thread in Multi-Turn Interaction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12922](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12922)
- **Authors**: Vardhan Dongre, Joseph Hsieh, Viet Dac Lai, Seunghyun Yoon, Trung Bui, Dilek Hakkani-Tür
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Long Context, LLM Reasoning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文提出通道转换理论解释LLM在多轮交互中丢失指令线索的现象，引入目标可达性比率(GAR)测量对目标token的注意力，发现不同架构在注意力通道关闭时表现出定性不同的失效模式。

<h3 id="16">[16] Sustaining AI safety: Control-theoretic external impossibility, intrinsic necessity, and structural requirements</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12963)
- **Authors**: James M. Mazzu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Control Theory, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 本文运用控制理论分析AI安全策略的结构性限制，证明了当系统效应超出有界外部控制可抵消的范围时，依赖外部执行的策略无法维持AI安全，剩余可行策略必须具有内在安全特性。

<h3 id="17">[17] Position: Agentic AI System Is a Foreseeable Pathway to AGI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12966](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12966)
- **Authors**: Junwei Liao, Shuai Li, Muning Wen, Jun Wang, Weinan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AGI Architecture, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文挑战了单纯通过扩大单一模型规模来实现AGI的观点，提出Agentic AI系统是掌握现实世界复杂任务分布的必要范式。作者通过理论推导证明，Agentic AI相比单一学习器在泛化能力和样本效率上具有指数级的优势。

<h3 id="18">[18] Retrieval is Cheap, Show Me the Code: Executable Multi-Hop Reasoning for Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12975](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12975)
- **Authors**: Jiashuo Sun, Jimeng Shi, Yixuan Xie, Saizhuo Wang, Jash Rajesh Parekh, Pengcheng Jiang, Zhiyi Shi, Jiajun Fan, Qinglong Zheng, Peiran Li, Shaowen Wang, Ge Liu, Jiawei Han
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Code Generation, Question Answering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GasolSun36/PyRAG)
- **Summary**: 本文提出PyRAG框架，将多跳RAG重新表述为程序合成与执行问题，将推理过程表示为可执行的Python程序。该方法通过执行提供确定性反馈，支持编译器引导的自修复，在五个QA基准测试上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="19">[19] Useful Memories Become Faulty When Continuously Updated by LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12978](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12978)
- **Authors**: Dylan Zhang, Yanshan Lin, Zhengkun Wu, Yihang Sun, Bingxuan Li, Dianqi Li, Hao Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文发现LLM生成的压缩记忆在持续更新过程中会出现质量退化，即使源自有用经验。研究表明保留原始情节轨迹比强制压缩更有效，可靠的智能体记忆应将原始情节作为一等证据对待。

<h3 id="20">[20] Retrieval-Augmented Tutoring for Algorithm Tracing and Problem-Solving in AI Education</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12988](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12988)
- **Authors**: Mragisha Jain, Tirth Bhatt, Griffin Pitts, Aum Pandya, Peter Brusilovsky, Narges Norouzi, Arto Hellas, Juho Leinonen, Bita Akram
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Education Technology, Question Answering
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文提出KITE，一个基于RAG的智能辅导系统，用于算法推理和问题求解的教学辅助。系统采用意图感知的苏格拉底式响应策略，从课程材料中检索相关信息，通过自动评估、专家评估和模拟学生对话验证了其有效性。

<h3 id="21">[21] MAP: A Map-then-Act Paradigm for Long-Horizon Interactive Agent Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13037](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13037)
- **Authors**: Yuxin Liu, Ziang Ye, Yueqing Sun, Mingye Zhu, Jinwei Xiao, Zhuowen Han, Qi GU, Xunliang Cai, Lei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Affordance Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出MAP（Map-then-Act）范式，将环境理解提前到执行之前，包含全局探索、任务特定映射和知识增强执行三个阶段。实验表明MAP在ARC-AGI-3基准上使前沿模型在25个游戏环境中的22个上突破了接近零的基线性能。

<h3 id="22">[22] An Agentic LLM-Based Framework for Population-Scale Mental Health Screening</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13046](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13046)
- **Authors**: Giuliano Lorenzoni, Paulo Alencar, Donald Cowan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Medical AI, Mental Health
- **Venue**: IEEE BigData 2025
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个基于智能体的LLM框架用于大规模心理健康筛查，每个阶段封装为LangChain智能体并由显式策略和代理评估引导。框架通过增量锁定和协调器智能体确保配置稳定，在抑郁症检测任务中展示了有效性。

<h3 id="23">[23] GRACE: Gradient-aligned Reasoning Data Curation for Efficient Post-training</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出GRACE方法，通过梯度对齐的方式对推理数据进行筛选，对每个推理步骤进行评分。该方法仅使用模型内部优化信号，无需外部奖励模型，在仅使用20%数据时达到全数据性能的108.8%。

<h3 id="24">[24] A Constraint Programming Approach for $n$-Day Lookahead Playoff Clinching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13142](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13142)

<h3 id="25">[25] Strikingness-Aware Evaluation for Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning</h3>

- **Authors**: Rikui Huang, Shengzhe Zhang, Wei Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Temporal Knowledge Graph, Knowledge Graph, Benchmark

<h3 id="26">[26] Formal Conjectures: An Open and Evolving Benchmark for Verified Discovery in Mathematics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13171)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="27">[27] Hierarchical Attacks for Multi-Modal Multi-Agent Reasoning</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出HAM³分层攻击框架，针对多模态多智能体系统在感知层、通信层和推理层进行攻击。实验在GQA基准上达到最高78.3%的攻击成功率，其中推理层攻击最为有效。

<h3 id="28">[28] An Agentic AI Framework with Large Language Models and Chain-of-Thought for UAV-Assisted Logistics Scheduling with Mobile Edge Computing</h3>

<h3 id="29">[29] Improving Code Translation with Syntax-Guided and Semantic-aware Preference Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13229](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13229)

- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026

<h3 id="30">[30] It's not the Language Model, it's the Tool: Deterministic Mediation for Scientific Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13245](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13245)

<h3 id="31">[31] Respecting Self-Uncertainty in On-Policy Self-Distillation for Efficient LLM Reasoning</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出EGRSD方法，通过教师熵置信门降低高熵token位置的权重，同时保持非零下界。还引入因果前瞻变体区分持续高熵和瞬时高熵位置，在Qwen3模型上推进了准确率-长度前沿。

<h3 id="32">[32] D-VLA: A High-Concurrency Distributed Asynchronous Reinforcement Learning Framework for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>

<h3 id="33">[33] Differentiable Learning of Lifted Action Schemas for Classical Planning</h3>

<h3 id="34">[34] What properties of reasoning supervision are associated with improved downstream model quality?</h3>

<h3 id="38">[38] VERA-MH: Validation of Ethical and Responsible AI in Mental Health</h3>

<h3 id="40">[40] Ego2World: Compiling Egocentric Cooking Videos into Executable Worlds for Belief-State Planning</h3>

<h3 id="44">[44] Cognifold: Always-On Proactive Memory via Cognitive Folding</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出跨窗口知识蒸馏框架，使学生编码器能够从在最具信息量窗口上训练的教师模型中学习潜在临床先验，在三个肺部CT数据集上将AUC提升了10.1-16.5个百分点。

<h3 id="88">[88] Pyramid Self-contrastive Learning Framework for Test-time Ultrasound Image Denoising</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12567) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Enhancement, Test-Time Adaptation
- **Summary**: 本文提出A2A纯测试时训练框架，通过金字塔潜空间中的自对比学习从打乱的子孔径中解耦解剖相似性和噪声随机性，实现单样本超声图像去噪，在仿真和体内实验中显著提升SNR和CNR。

<h3 id="89">[89] Active Sensing with Meta-Reinforcement Learning for Emitter Localization from RF Observations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12569](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12569) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文将GNSS干扰源定位建模为主动感知问题，提出结合高维RF感知和深度强化学习的框架，使用DQN和PPO在仿真环境中实现了80.1%的定位成功率。

<h3 id="90">[90] VideoSEAL: Mitigating Evidence Misalignment in Agentic Long Video Understanding by Decoupling Answer Authority</h3>

<h3 id="91">[91] Improving Diffusion Posterior Samplers with Lagged Temporal Corrections for Image Restoration</h3>

<h3 id="92">[92] DistractMIA: Black-Box Membership Inference on Vision-Language Models via Semantic Distraction</h3>

- **Authors**: Hongyi Tang, Zhihao Zhu, Yi Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

<h3 id="93">[93] Are Compact Rationales Free? Measuring Tile Selection Headroom in Frozen WSI-MIL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12575](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12575) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyun Do Jung, Jungwon Choi, Soojung Choi, Yujin Oh, Hwiyoung Kim
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV

- **Summary**: 该论文研究全切片图像(WSI)多实例学习(MIL)分类器的后验解释问题，提出FOCI方法用于在冻结的MIL模型上识别紧凑的、输出一致的图块子集作为预测依据。实验表明，Transformer和多分支注意力聚合器能够接受紧凑的推理依据，FOCI在TransMIL上将最小充分K图块计数减少了32-56%。

<h3 id="94">[94] Towards Robust Federated Multimodal Graph Learning under Modality Heterogeneity</h3>

- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文研究冻结WSI-MIL分类器的后验理据高亮问题，提出FOCI轻量级理据读出层，发现紧凑理据依赖于选择余量，Transformer和多分支注意力聚合器可接受紧凑理据，而注意力池化基线进入选择饱和状态。

<h3 id="95">[95] 3D Primitives are a Spatial Language for VLMs</h3>

<h3 id="96">[96] The critical slowing down in diffusion models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12597](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12597) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="97">[97] Training LLMs with Reinforcement Learning for Intent-Aware Personalized Question Answering</h3>

<h3 id="98">[98] Learning to Decide with AI Assistance under Human-Alignment</h3>

<h3 id="99">[99] Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation via Peer Successes and Failures</h3>

<h3 id="101">[101] ODRPO: Ordinal Decompositions of Discrete Rewards for Robust Policy Optimization</h3>

<h3 id="106">[106] Modeling Heterophily in Multiplex Graphs: An Adaptive Approach for Node Classification</h3>

<h3 id="107">[107] Do Fair Models Reason Fairly? Counterfactual Explanation Consistency for Procedural Fairness in Credit Decisions</h3>

<h3 id="108">[108] MMCL-Bench: Multimodal Context Learning from Visual Rules, Procedures, and Evidence</h3>

<h3 id="110">[110] Controllable Quantum Memory Capacity in Quantum Reservoir Networks with Tunable partial-SWAPs</h3>

<h3 id="111">[111] The End Justifies the Mean: A Linear Ranking Rule for Proportional Sequential Decisions</h3>

<h3 id="113">[113] Grid-Orch: An LLM-Powered Orchestrator for Distribution Grid Simulation and Analytics</h3>

<h3 id="114">[114] Large Language Models for Agentic NetOps and AIOps: Architectures, Evaluation, and Safety</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12729](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12729) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="115">[115] From Generalist to Specialist Representation</h3>

<h3 id="116">[116] What Do You Think I Think? Accounting for Human Beliefs Using Second-Order Theory of Mind</h3>

<h3 id="117">[117] CoT-Guard: Small Models for Strong Monitoring</h3>

<h3 id="118">[118] Simulating Students or Sycophantic Problem Solving? On Misconception Faithfulness of LLM Simulators</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文研究大语言模型作为学生模拟器时是否能真实地保持错误概念驱动的信念状态。作者提出了一个评估框架和选择性翻转分数(SFS)指标，发现现有模型表现出"阿谀奉承"的失败模式——更像是问题解决者而非持有错误概念的学生。作者还开发了包含监督微调、偏好优化和强化学习的后训练流程来改善这一问题。

<h3 id="119">[119] Uncovering Symmetry Transfer in Large Language Models via Layer-Peeled Optimization</h3>

- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; stat.ML

<h3 id="120">[120] Multi-Quantile Regression for Extreme Precipitation Downscaling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12762](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12762) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hamed Najafi, Gareth Lagerwall, Jayantha Obeysekera, Jason Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Deep Learning, Extreme Event Detection
- **Summary**: 本文提出Q-SRDRN多分位数超分辨率网络用于降水降尺度，通过在多个分位数上使用pinball损失训练，显著提升了对极端降水事件的检测能力，在佛罗里达数据集上将200mm/天以上事件的检测率从4.2%提升至75.7%。

<h3 id="121">[121] WriteSAE: Sparse Autoencoders for Recurrent State</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12770) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jack Young
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Interpretability, State Space Model

- **Summary**: 本文提出WriteSAE，首个能够分解和编辑状态空间模型及混合循环语言模型矩阵缓存写入的稀疏自编码器，通过将解码器原子分解为原生写入形状，实现了对Mamba-2、RWKV-7等模型的可解释性分析和行为干预。

<h3 id="122">[122] Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff Attention for Multi-Objective Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12771](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12771) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alejandro Murillo-Gonzalez, Mahmoud Ali, Lantao Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY; math.OC
- **Tags**: Multi-Objective Optimization, Reinforcement Learning, Robotics
- **Venue**: RSS 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出自适应平滑切比雪夫注意力框架用于多目标强化学习，通过冲突驱动的控制器动态调节优化景观的曲率，使智能体能够在目标对齐时逼近精确的非凸标量化，在梯度冲突时弹性回退到稳定的平滑近似。

<h3 id="123">[123] Emergent and Subliminal Misalignment Through the Lens of Data-Mediated Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12798](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12798) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Baris Askin, Muhammed Ustaomeroglu, Anupam Nayak, Gauri Joshi, Guannan Qu, Carlee Joe-Wong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Fine-Tuning, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文研究了在有害数据集上微调LLM时出现的涌现性错位现象，发现错位行为是数据介导的迁移现象，与数据集结构、任务难度和预训练分布相关，并比较了离策略和在线策略蒸馏在传递错位中的作用。

<h3 id="124">[124] Discrete MeanFlow: One-Step Generation via Conditional Transition Kernels</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12805) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="125">[125] Correcting Influence: Unboxing LLM Outputs with Orthogonal Latent Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12809](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12809) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shixing Yu, Promit Ghosal, Kyra Gan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Medical AI, Data Attribution
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种新的框架，通过稀疏自编码器学习独立潜在特征来实现LLM输出的token级影响归因。针对先前方法假设token独立性的局限，作者引入基于雅可比向量积的方法将潜在归因传播回输入空间。在医疗基准测试上的实验表明，该方法能识别出稀疏且可解释的影响token集合。

<h3 id="126">[126] REALISTA: Realistic Latent Adversarial Attacks that Elicit LLM Hallucinations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12813](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12813) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Interpretability, Data Attribution

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种通过潜在中介方法推断LLM token级影响力的框架，利用稀疏自编码器学习近似独立的潜在特征基，通过雅可比向量积将潜在归因传播回输入空间，实现了对医疗预测任务中训练数据影响力的精确识别。

<h3 id="127">[127] Training Large Language Models to Predict Clinical Events</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12817) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="128">[128] Mechanism Plausibility in Generative Agent-Based Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12824](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12824) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="129">[129] Orthrus: Memory-Efficient Parallel Token Generation via Dual-View Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12825) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="130">[130] FRAME: Forensic Routing and Adaptive Multi-path Evidence Fusion for Image Manipulation Detection</h3>

<h3 id="131">[131] GraphIP-Bench: How Hard Is It to Steal a Graph Neural Network, and Can We Stop It?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12827](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12827) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="132">[132] Bayesian Model Merging</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了贝叶斯模型合并(BMM)框架，通过双层优化将多个任务特定模型合并为单一模型。该方法利用锚模型的先验和贝叶斯优化搜索模块特定超参数，在视觉和语言任务上均优于现有方法。

<h3 id="133">[133] AssemblyBench: Physics-Aware Assembly of Complex Industrial Objects</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文介绍了AssemblyBench数据集，包含2,789个工业对象的多模态说明书、3D零件模型和装配轨迹。同时提出了AssemblyDyno模型，联合预测装配顺序和零件装配轨迹。

<h3 id="135">[135] PRISM: Perinuclear Ring-based Image Segmentation Method for Acute Lymphoblastic Leukemia Classification</h3>

<h3 id="136">[136] ChipMATE: Multi-Agent Training via Reinforcement Learning for Enhanced RTL Generation</h3>

<h3 id="138">[138] Quantifying LLM Safety Degradation Under Repeated Attacks Using Survival Analysis</h3>

<h3 id="139">[139] EcoGEO: Trajectory-Aware Evidence Ecosystems for Web-Enabled LLM Search Agents</h3>

<h3 id="140">[140] RISED: A Pre-Deployment Safety Evaluation Framework for Clinical AI Decision-Support Systems</h3>

- **Authors**: Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CY; stat.AP
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Safety Evaluation, Model Evaluation

- **Summary**: 本文提出了RISED框架，从可靠性、包容性、敏感性、公平性和可部署性五个维度对临床AI决策支持系统进行部署前评估。该框架能检测到传统聚合指标无法发现的部署风险。

<h3 id="141">[141] Data Difficulty and the Generalization--Extrapolation Tradeoff in LLM Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12906](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12906) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyuan Liu, Tinghong Chen, Xinghan Li, Yifei Wang, Jingzhao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fine-Tuning, Data Selection, LLM Training
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了LLM微调中数据难度的作用，发现不存在普适的最优难度水平，最优难度随数据预算增加而向更难数据偏移。通过泛化差距和外推差距的权衡解释了这一现象。

<h3 id="142">[142] Embodied Multi-Agent Coordination by Aligning World Models Through Dialogue</h3>

- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Embodied AI, World Model
- **Summary**: 本文研究了LLM具身智能体是否通过对话实现世界模型对齐。实验发现对话减少了40-83个百分点的动作冲突，但任务成功率下降，揭示了表面协调与真实世界模型对齐之间的差距。

<h3 id="143">[143] AgentLens: Revealing The Lucky Pass Problem in SWE-Agent Evaluation</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文介绍了AgentLens框架，用于SWE智能体轨迹的过程级评估。研究发现10.7%的通过轨迹是"幸运通过"，质量评分可使模型排名发生最多5个位置的变化。

<h3 id="144">[144] Anatomy-Slot: Unsupervised Anatomical Factorization for Homologous Bilateral Reasoning in Retinal Diagnosis</h3>

<h3 id="145">[145] AuraMask: An Extensible Pipeline for Developing Aesthetic Anti-Facial Recognition Image Filters</h3>

<h3 id="146">[146] CRePE: Curved Ray Expectation Positional Encoding for Unified-Camera-Controlled Video Generation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="147">[147] The Expressivity Boundary of Probabilistic Circuits: A Comparison with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12940) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyu Zhao, Xuejie Liu, Muhan Zhang, Anji Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Generative Model, Probabilistic Inference
- **Summary**: 本文在统一的自回归框架下比较了概率电路(PCs)和大语言模型(LLMs)，发现了两个瓶颈：输出瓶颈（概率空间的凸组合难以表示语言的尖锐分布）和上下文编码瓶颈（结构可分解PCs的表达能力受限于固定路由结构）。作者证明了可分解PCs比结构可分解PCs更具表达能力。

<h3 id="148">[148] When Should an AI Workflow Release? Always-Valid Inference for Black-Box Generate-Verify Systems</h3>

- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ME
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Code Generation, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 本文为LLM工作流中的生成-验证循环提出了一种始终有效的发布包装器，通过构建硬负例参考池、校准评估分数并使用e过程累积证据，控制在不可行任务上错误发布的概率。在MBPP+编程代理案例研究中，该方法减少了过早错误发布的情况。

<h3 id="149">[149] Seg-Agent: Test-Time Multimodal Reasoning for Training-Free Language-Guided Segmentation</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出Seg-Agent，一个完全免训练的语言引导分割框架，采用显式多模态链式推理。该方法通过Set-of-Mark视觉提示构建交互式视觉推理循环（生成、选择、精炼），使多模态大语言模型能够在视觉域而非仅文本域进行空间关系推理，性能可媲美基于训练的方法。

<h3 id="150">[150] AdaFocus: Adaptive Relevance-Diversity Sampling with Zero-Cache Look-back for Efficient Long Video Understanding</h3>

<h3 id="151">[151] Controlling Logical Collapse in LLMs via Algebraic Ontology Projection over F2</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Interpretability, LLM Reasoning, Logical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文引入代数本体投影(AOP)，将LLM隐藏状态投影到伽罗瓦域F2中，在未见概念对上实现高达93.33%的零样本包含准确率。作者发现了后期层崩溃现象，并证明系统提示与指令微调的结合可以防止这种逻辑一致性退化。

<h3 id="152">[152] Revisiting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards from a Contrastive Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12969) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Feng Zhang, Xinhong Ma, Ziqiang Dong, Xi Leng, Jianfei Zhao, Xin Sun, Yang Yang, Guanjun Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文从对比视角重新审视RLVR，将GRPO重新表述为判别式的加权正负样本分数差，揭示了似然错位评分和分数不敏感信用分配两个结构限制。作者提出ConSPO框架，使用长度归一化序列对数概率和InfoNCE风格目标，在数学推理基准测试上持续优于RLVR基线。

<h3 id="153">[153] CoRe-Gen: Robust Spectrum-to-Structure Generation under Imperfect Fingerprint Conditions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12980](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12980) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianbo Liu, Chixiang Lu, Jing Hao, Hengyu Zhang, Lifei Wang, Haibo Jiang, Xiaojuan Qi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Drug Discovery, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出CoRe-Gen方法，用于在非完美指纹条件下从串联质谱生成分子结构。该方法通过合成光谱预训练、频率感知指纹腐蚀和结构感知自回归解码，解决了训练与部署之间的条件不匹配问题，在NPLIB1基准上达到了新的最优结果。

<h3 id="154">[154] Protocol-Driven Development: Governing Generated Software Through Invariants and Evidence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12981](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12981) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun He, Deying Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Program Synthesis, Software Engineering, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 本文提出协议驱动开发(PDD)模型，其中主要软件制品是机器可执行的协议而非实现代码。协议定义了结构、行为和操作不变量，实现只有满足协议并产生可验证的合规证据链时才被接纳，为自动化软件工程提供了治理层。

<h3 id="155">[155] Not Just RLHF: Why Alignment Alone Won't Fix Multi-Agent Sycophancy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12991](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12991) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adarsh Kumarappan, Ananya Mujoo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Alignment, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 本文研究了多智能体管道中的奉承行为，发现预训练基础模型与其指令变体表现出相同的替换模式，平均屈服率更高，这反驳了将此漏洞归因于RLHF诱导的奉承行为的观点。通过激活修补，作者将损坏定位到狭窄的中层窗口，并发现压力抑制了清晰推理特征而非激活新的奉承电路。

<h3 id="156">[156] Amortized Guidance for Image Inpainting with Pretrained Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13010) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; eess.SY; math.OC
- **Tags**: Image Inpainting, Diffusion Model, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出Amortized Inpainting with Diffusion (AID)，保持预训练扩散主干固定，离线训练小型可复用引导模块。作者推导了辅助高斯公式并证明解决该随机问题可恢复最优确定性引导场，在多个数据集和掩码类型上实现了更好的质量-速度权衡。

<h3 id="157">[157] Rethinking Efficient Graph Coarsening via a Non-Selfishness Principle</h3>

- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Optimization, Graph Learning

- **Summary**: 本文提出NOPE方法，基于非自私原则优先考虑邻域集体干扰进行图粗化，实现线性内存消耗和近线性计算复杂度。快速变体NOPE*在高阶节点上进一步降低计算瓶颈，相比基线实现1-3个数量级的加速，同时保持学习性能。

<h3 id="158">[158] Understanding and Accelerating the Training of Masked Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13026) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL

- **Summary**: 本文分析了掩码扩散语言模型训练缓慢的原因，发现语言局部性偏差是主要因素。作者提出钟形时间采样训练策略，在One Billion Word基准上使MDMs达到相同验证负对数似然的速度提升约4倍，同时保持最终性能。

<h3 id="159">[159] FeatCal: Feature Calibration for Post-Merging Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13030) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Merging, Transfer Learning, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文通过特征漂移研究模型合并的性能差距，提出FeatCal方法，使用小型校准集按前向顺序逐层校准合并模型权重。该方法在CLIP和GLUE基准上优于现有后合并校准基线，具有更好的样本效率和更低的校准成本。

<h3 id="160">[160] CoGE: Sim-to-Real Online Geometric Estimation for Monocular Colonoscopy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13038) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出CoGE框架，用于单目结肠镜检查中的在线几何估计。该方法基于Retinex理论提出光照感知监督模块，基于小波分解提出结构感知感知模块，仅使用模拟数据训练即可在模拟和真实场景中达到最先进的几何估计性能。

<h3 id="161">[161] No Attack Required: Semantic Fuzzing for Specification Violations in Agent Skills</h3>

<h3 id="162">[162] Revealing the Gap in Human and VLM Scene Perception through Counterfactual Semantic Saliency</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了反事实语义显著性(CSS)框架，通过测量对象移除引起的语义变化来量化视觉语言模型与人类场景感知的差异。研究发现模型存在对大物体、中心物体和高显著性物体的过度依赖偏差，与人类感知存在显著差距。

<h3 id="165">[165] When Absolute State Fails: Evaluating Proprioceptive Encodings for Robust Manipulation</h3>

<h3 id="171">[171] Vividh-ASR: A Complexity-Tiered Benchmark and Optimization Dynamics for Robust Indic Speech Recognition</h3>

<h3 id="177">[177] Towards Long-horizon Embodied Agents with Tool-Aligned Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>

<h3 id="178">[178] MLGIB: Multi-Label Graph Information Bottleneck for Expressive and Robust Message Passing</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Benchmark, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 本文提出DESBench基准，用于评估层次化事件驱动调度中的代理协调，涵盖多时间尺度决策、部分可观测性和动态耦合约束。通过对集中式、层次式、异构式和全息式四种协调范式的评估，揭示了协调设计在复杂环境中的结构性权衡。

<h3 id="183">[183] When Does Hierarchy Help? Benchmarking Agent Coordination in Event-Driven Industrial Scheduling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13172](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13172) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziqi Wang, Yuhao Yang, Zhiwei Ling, Wenzhuo Qian, Hailiang Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Coordination, Benchmark, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了DESBench，一个用于评估层次化事件驱动调度中智能体协调能力的基准测试。该基准基于工业调度中的共享离散事件驱动环境，捕捉多时间尺度决策、部分可观测性和动态耦合约束。研究比较了四种协调范式（集中式、层次式、异构式和整体式），揭示了它们在鲁棒性、效率和约束满足方面的权衡关系。

<h3 id="184">[184] CLIP Tricks You: Training-free Token Pruning for Efficient Pixel Grounding in Large VIsion-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13178) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sangin Lee, Yukyung Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Inference, Visual Grounding
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sejong-rcv/LiteLVLM)
- **Summary**: 本文发现CLIP视觉-文本相似度排序在像素定位任务中存在问题，提出LiteLVLM训练无关的文本引导令牌剪枝策略。通过反转CLIP相似度排序，该方法有效保留了覆盖参考区域的视觉令牌，在保持90%原始性能的同时实现了22%的加速和2.3倍的内存减少。

<h3 id="185">[185] Stable Attention Response for Reliable Precipitation Nowcasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13181) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Penghui Wen, Zexin Hu, Sen Zhang, Patrick Filippi, Xiaogang Zhu, Allen Benter, Thomas Bishop, Zhiyong Wang, Kun Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Time Series Forecasting, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 本文发现注意力响应能量的跨样本不稳定性是降水临近预报不可靠性的重要来源，提出HARECast框架通过组级正则化目标稳定注意力响应能量。该方法在SEVIR和MeteNet基准上取得了最先进的性能，适用于单模态和多模态预报架构。

<h3 id="186">[186] N-vium: Mixture-of-Exits Transformer for Accelerated Exact Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13190](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13190) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aleksander Lorenc, Frédéric Berdoz, Joël Mathys, Roger Wattenhofer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Transformer Architecture, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 本文提出N-vium混合退出Transformer，通过在多个深度附加预测头并定义下一词分布为这些退出的学习混合，实现了标准硬件上的计算并行化。该方法在1.5B参数规模上实现了57.9%的时钟加速，且不损失困惑度。

<h3 id="187">[187] ECG-NAT: A Self-supervised Neighborhood Attention Transformer for Multi-lead Electrocardiogram Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13194) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mahsa Gazeran, Sayvan Soleymanbaigi, Fatemeh Daneshfar, Amjad Seyedi, Fardin Akhlaghian Tab
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Self-Supervised Learning, Biosignal Processing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Mahsagazeran/ECG-NAT)
- **Summary**: 本文提出ECG-NAT自监督邻域注意力Transformer用于多导联心电图分类，采用两阶段方法：生成式预训练重建部分掩码的ECG信号，判别式微调结合监督对比损失和交叉熵损失。该方法仅需1%标注数据即可达到88.1%准确率，在低资源场景下表现优异。

<h3 id="188">[188] McCast: Memory-Guided Latent Drift Correction for Long-Horizon Precipitation Nowcasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13197](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13197) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Penghui Wen, Yu Luo, Lintao Wang, Mengwei He, Patrick Filippi, Thomas Francis Bishop, Zhiyong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Time Series Forecasting, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出McCast记忆引导潜在漂移校正方法，通过漂移校正记忆库主动校正自回归潜在演化轨迹。该方法在SEVIR和MeteNet基准上取得了最先进的性能，特别是在长期预报场景中表现突出。

<h3 id="189">[189] STAR: Semantic-Temporal Adaptive Representation Learning for Few-Shot Action Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13202](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13202) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongli Liu, Yu Wang, Shengjie Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, Action Recognition, Video Understanding
- **Venue**: TCSVT
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HongliLiu1/STAR-main)
- **Summary**: 本文提出STAR语义-时序自适应表示学习框架，通过时序语义注意力机制实现帧级跨模态对齐，并引入语义时序原型细化器集成语义引导的Mamba块。该方法在五个少样本动作识别基准上取得了显著提升，在1-shot设置下SSv2-Full数据集上提升8.1%。

<h3 id="190">[190] ReTool-Video: Recursive Tool-Using Video Agents with Meta-Augmented Tool Grounding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13228](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13228) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiao Liu, Nayu Liu, Junnan Zhu, Ruirui Chen, Guohui Xiang, Changjian Wang, Kaiwen Wei, Rongzhen Li, Jiang Zhong
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, LLM Agent, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出ReTool-Video递归工具使用方法，构建了包含134个工具的MetaAug-Video工具库，支持双级访问结构化视频信息和原始模态证据。该方法通过递归接地将高层视频意图转化为可执行工具链，在MVBench、MLVU和Video-MME基准上持续优于强基线。

<h3 id="191">[191] Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13230](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13230) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Liu, Kechen Jiao, Chunyang Xiao, Runsong Zhao, Junhao Ruan, Bei Li, Jiahao Liu, Qifan Wang, Xin Chen, Jingang Wang, Tong Xiao, JingBo Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Training, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出教师引导策略优化(TGPO)算法，通过利用以学生rollout为条件的教师预测来提供密集方向指导，解决了标准逆向KL在师生分布差异显著时无法产生有意义改进的问题。该方法在复杂推理基准上显著优于标准基线。

<h3 id="192">[192] Compact Latent Manifold Translation: A Parameter-Efficient Foundation Model for Cross-Modal and Cross-Frequency Physiological Signal Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13248](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13248) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bo Cui, Xiaowen Song, Yaowen Zhang, Shunzhe Zhang, B.J.F. van Beijnum, Monique Tabak, Ying Wang
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Foundation Model, Time Series Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出紧凑潜在流形翻译(CLMT)框架，通过分层残差向量量化将异构信号解耦为隔离的离散潜在流形，并使用上下文提示潜在翻译器实现跨模态映射。该0.09B参数模型在跨模态PPG到ECG合成中将R峰检测F1分数从0.37提升到0.83，在跨频率超分辨率中达到0.9956的皮尔逊相关系数。

<h3 id="193">[193] X-Restormer++: 1st Place Solution for the UG2+ CVPR 2026 All-Weather Restoration Challenge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13258) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youwei Pan, Leilei Cao, Yingfang Zhu, Fengjie Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Restoration, Image Enhancement, Benchmark
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文提出了UG2+ CVPR 2026全天气图像恢复挑战赛的冠军方案，基于X-Restormer框架进行了多项改进，包括引入空间自适应输入缩放机制、提出梯度引导边缘感知损失函数，并大幅扩展训练数据集。

<h3 id="194">[194] "It became a self-fulfilling prophecy": How Lived Experiences are Entangled with AI Predictions in Menstrual Cycle Tracking Apps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13261](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13261) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wendy Zhou, Pelin Karaturhan, Alexandra Weilenmann, Jichen Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, AI Ethics, Healthcare Monitoring
- **Summary**: 本文通过访谈和群体自我民族志方法，研究了月经周期追踪应用中AI预测功能如何与用户的生活体验相互交织，发现用户会根据AI预测理解自身经历，但界面设计和AI解释未能支持用户对这种纠缠关系的批判性思考。

<h3 id="195">[195] Utility-Oriented Visual Evidence Selection for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13277) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文从信息论角度重新定义多模态RAG中的视觉证据选择问题，提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过轻量级多模态模型高效估计证据效用，在多个基准测试上超越了现有RAG方法。

<h3 id="196">[196] The Readability Spectrum: Patterns, Issues, and Prompt Effects in LLM-Generated Code</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13280](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13280) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hengzhi Ye, Fengyuan Ran, Weiwei Xu, Minghui Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Code Quality Assessment, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究了LLM生成代码的可读性问题，建立了综合可读性模型，发现LLM生成的代码整体可读性与人类代码相当，但呈现出独特的可读性问题模式，且提示工程对可读性的影响有限。

<h3 id="197">[197] Delightful Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13287](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13287) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ian Osband
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Exploration Strategy, Multi-Armed Bandit
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Delight-gated exploration (DE)探索算法，仅在预期改进与惊讶度的乘积超过阈值时才执行探索动作，在多种强化学习设置中相比Thompson Sampling和ε-greedy表现出更低的遗憾增长。

<h3 id="198">[198] IndicMedDialog: A Parallel Multi-Turn Medical Dialogue Dataset for Accessible Healthcare in Indic Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13292) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG

<h3 id="199">[199] CANTANTE: Optimizing Agentic Systems via Contrastive Credit Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13295) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.MA

<h3 id="200">[200] What Limits Vision-and-Language Navigation ?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13328](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13328) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yunheng Wang, Yuetong Fang, Taowen Wang, Lusong Li, Kun Liu, Junzhe Xu, Zizhao Yuan, Yixiao Feng, Jiaxi Zhang, Wei Lu, Zecui Zeng, Renjing Xu

<h3 id="201">[201] Tracing Persona Vectors Through LLM Pretraining</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13329](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13329) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Viktor Moskvoretskii, Dominik Glandorf, Jorge Medina Moreira, Tanja Käser, Robert West
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, LLM Alignment, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文追踪了LLM预训练过程中人格向量的形成过程，发现代表邪恶或谄媚等特征的人格向量在预训练极早期（0.22%进度）就已形成，并在整个预训练过程中持续进行几何和语义上的精炼。

<h3 id="202">[202] Stylized Text-to-Motion Generation via Hypernetwork-Driven Low-Rank Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13333) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个轻量级的风格条件化框架，通过超网络生成的LoRA参数动态调制预训练扩散模型，实现了文本到动作的风格化生成，在保持高效的同时支持未见过的风格泛化。

<h3 id="203">[203] Inducing Overthink: Hierarchical Genetic Algorithm-based DoS Attack on Black-Box Large Language Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13338](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13338) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuqiang Wang, Wei Cao, Jiaqi Weng, Jialing Tao, Licheng Pan, Hui Xue, Zhixuan Chu

<h3 id="204">[204] Probing Persona-Dependent Preferences in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13339) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oscar Gilg, Pierre Beckmann, Daniel Paleka, Patrick Butlin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, LLM Alignment, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/oscar-gilg/Preferences)
- **Summary**: 本文通过训练线性探针研究LLM中人格相关偏好的内部表示，发现存在一个真正的偏好向量，可以追踪和操控模型在不同提示和情境下的偏好选择，且该偏好表示在不同人格之间很大程度上是共享的。

<h3 id="205">[205] AI Harness Engineering: A Runtime Substrate for Foundation-Model Software Agents</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Software Engineering, Agent Architecture

<h3 id="206">[206] Constitutional Governance in Metric Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13362) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="207">[207] A Horn extension of DL-Lite with NL data complexity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13367) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="208">[208] Query-Conditioned Test-Time Self-Training for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13369](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13369) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chaehee Song, Minseok Seo, Yeeun Seong, Doyi Kim, Changick Kim

<h3 id="209">[209] GRIP-VLM: Group-Relative Importance Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13375](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13375) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Model Compression, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出GRIP-VLM，一个基于强化学习的视觉语言模型视觉token剪枝框架。该方法将token剪枝建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过群组相对策略优化实现离散选择空间的直接探索，在保持准确率的同时实现最高15%的推理加速。

<h3 id="210">[210] LLMs as annotators of credibility assessment in Danish asylum decisions: evaluating classification performance and errors beyond aggregated metrics</h3>

<h3 id="211">[211] Continual Learning with Multilingual Foundation Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13415) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="212">[212] Towards a holistic understanding of Selection Bias for Causal Effect Identification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13430](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13430) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Selection Bias
- **Summary**: 本文研究选择偏差下平均处理效应的可识别性问题。研究提供了ATE可识别的充要条件，利用概率类的弱假设来刻画倾向得分和选择概率，相比现有工作提供了更全面的理解和更弱的条件。

<h3 id="213">[213] Q-Flow: Stable and Expressive Reinforcement Learning with Flow-Based Policy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13435](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13435) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: JaeHyeok Doo, Byeongguk Jeon, Seonghyeon Ye, Kimin Lee, Minjoon Seo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Flow Matching, Offline RL
- **Summary**: 本文提出Q-Flow框架，利用流模型作为强化学习策略以实现高表达能力。该方法通过流动力学的确定性特性将终端轨迹价值显式传播到中间潜在状态，实现了稳定策略优化，在离线学习设置中平均提升10.6个百分点。

<h3 id="214">[214] CUBic: Coordinated Unified Bimanual Perception and Control Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13452](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13452) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Wang, Pengxiang Ding, Jingkai Xu, Donglin Wang, Zhaoxin Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Calibration, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个发现隐藏校准错误模式的诊断框架，通过学习校准感知的输入空间表示来估计局部校准误差。研究发现LLM中普遍存在输入依赖的校准异质性，并展示了该方法在校正系统校准错误区域方面的有效性。

<h3 id="215">[215] Discovery of Hidden Miscalibration Regimes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13484](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13484) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Katarzyna Kobalczyk, Mihaela van der Schaar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Robotics, Embodied AI, Multi-Agent Coordination
- **Summary**: 本文提出CUBic，一个统一的双臂机器人感知与控制框架。该方法学习共享的token化表示来桥接感知与控制，使独立性和协调性从结构中自然涌现，在RoboTwin基准上显著提升了协调准确性和任务成功率。

<h3 id="216">[216] Many-Shot CoT-ICL: Making In-Context Learning Truly Learn</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13511](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13511) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tsz Ting Chung, Lemao Liu, Mo Yu, Dit-Yan Yeung
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Chain-of-Thought, LLM Reasoning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究多示例链式思维上下文学习的缩放行为，发现标准多示例规则不适用于推理任务。研究提出曲率演示选择方法，将长上下文窗口重构为上下文测试时学习的结构化课程，在几何任务上获得最高5.42个百分点的提升。

<h3 id="217">[217] ArcVQ-VAE: A Spherical Vector Quantization Framework with ArcCosine Additive Margin</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13517](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13517) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaeyung Kim, YoungJoon Yoo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Image Synthesis, Generative Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/goals4292/ArcVQ-VAE)
- **Summary**: 本文提出ArcVQ-VAE，一种引入球面角度边际先验的向量量化框架。该方法通过约束码本向量在欧氏球内并增强角度可分性，提高了码本利用率和潜在空间覆盖率，在图像重建和生成任务上取得了竞争性性能。

<h3 id="218">[218] Towards Unified Surgical Scene Understanding:Bridging Reasoning and Grounding via MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13530](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13530) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jincai Huang, Shihao Zou, Yuchen Guo, Jingjing Li, Wei Ji, Kai Wang, Shanshan Wang, Weixin Si
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Surgical Video Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文提出SurgMLLM，一个统一手术场景理解框架，通过多模态大语言模型桥接高层推理和底层视觉定位。该框架端到端训练，在相位识别、三元组识别和分割任务上均显著超越现有方法。

<h3 id="219">[219] HLS-Seek: QoR-Aware Code Generation for High-Level Synthesis via Proxy Comparative Reward Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13536](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13536) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qingyun Zou, Feng Yu, Hongshi Tan, Yao Chen, Bingsheng He, WengFai Wong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, HLS, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出HLS-Seek，一个QoR感知的NL-to-HLS代码生成框架。该方法使用代理比较奖励模型替代昂贵的综合循环强化学习，在7B参数下实现81.5%的语法正确率，超越GPT-5.1等前沿模型。

<h3 id="220">[220] Temper and Tilt Lead to SLOP: Reward Hacking Mitigation with Inference-Time Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13537](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13537) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ye Wang, Jing Liu, Toshiaki Koike-Akino
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Reward Hacking
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种推理时对齐技术SLOP，结合生成式奖励模型集成来缓解奖励黑客问题。该方法引入参考模型温度调整和校准算法，在保持对齐性能的同时提高了鲁棒性。

<h3 id="221">[221] Locale-Conditioned Few-Shot Prompting Mitigates Demonstration Regurgitation in On-Device PII Substitution with Small Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13538](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13538) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anuj Sadani, Deepak Kumar
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Privacy, Small Language Model, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个完全在设备的PII替换流水线，使用小语言模型生成类型保持的假值。研究发现语言条件化的旋转少样本提示可以缓解演示复述问题，但混合方法在下游NER任务上的效果不如基于规则的生成器。

<h3 id="222">[222] Decoupled and Divergence-Conditioned Prompt for Multi-domain Dynamic Graph Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13540](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13540) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haonan Yuan, Qingyun Sun, Junhua Shi, Xingcheng Fu, Jianxin Li, Philip S. Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Learning, Transfer Learning, Dynamic Graph
- **Summary**: 本文提出DyGFM，一个基于解耦和散度条件提示的多域动态图基础模型。该方法通过语义-时序解耦的双分支预训练和散度感知专家选择机制，在节点分类和链接预测任务上持续超越12个最先进基线。

<h3 id="223">[223] AttenA+: Rectifying Action Inequality in Robotic Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13548) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daojie Peng, Fulong Ma, Jiahang Cao, Qiang Zhang, Xupeng Xie, Jian Guo, Ping Luo, Andrew F. Luo, Boyu Zhou, Jun Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Foundation Model, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出AttenA+，一个架构无关的框架，通过速度驱动的动作注意力优先处理运动学关键片段。该方法自然地将模型学习能力与操作的物理需求对齐，在Libero和RoboTwin基准上显著提升了最先进模型的性能上限。

<h3 id="224">[224] Self-Supervised On-Policy Reinforcement Learning via Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13554](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13554) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Asim Osman, Sasha Abramowitz, Mark Bergh, Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob, Ruan John de Kock, Omayma Mahjoub, Oussama Hidaoui, Noah De Nicola, Arnol Manuel Fokam, Felix Chalumeau, Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa, Siddarth Singh, Refiloe Shabe, Juan Claude Formanek, Simon Verster Du Toit, Arnu Pretorius
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Contrastive Learning, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出对比近端策略优化(CPPO)，首个在策略对比强化学习算法。该方法直接从对比Q值推导策略优势并通过标准PPO目标优化，在18个任务中有14个显著超越之前的CRL基线，并在12个任务上匹配或超越使用手工密集奖励的PPO。

<h3 id="225">[225] Generating synthetic computed tomography for radiotherapy: SynthRAD2025 challenge report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13555](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13555) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Viktor Rogowski, Maarten L. Terpstra, Niklas Wahl, Florian Kamp, Erik van der Bijl, Arthur Jr. Galapon, Christopher Kurz, Bowen Xin, Zhengxiang Sun, Hollie Min, Gregg Belous, Jason Dowling, Yan Xia, Siyuan Mei, Fuxin Fan, Arthur Longuefosse, Javier Sequeiro Gonzalez, Miguel Diaz Benito, Alvaro Garcia Martin, Fabien Baldacci, Valentin Boussot, Cédric Hémon, Jean-Claude Nunes, Jean-Louis Dillenseger, Zhiyuan Zhang, Jinghua Cai, Han Bing, Tan Zuopeng, Ricardo Brioso, Daniele Loiacono, Guillaume Landry, Adrian Thummerer, Matteo Maspero
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Synthesis, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文报告了SynthRAD2025挑战赛，评估了从MRI或CBCT生成合成CT用于放射治疗规划的方法。研究涉及2,362名患者的多中心数据，发现深度学习生成的合成CT具有临床相关性，尤其是CBCT到CT的转换效果更优，同时指出基于剂量的评估对临床验证至关重要。

<h3 id="226">[226] Dynamical Predictive Modelling of Cardiovascular Disease Progression Post-Myocardial Infarction via ECG-Trained Artificial Intelligence Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13568](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13568) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Riccardo Cavarra, Lupo Lovatelli, Shaheim Ogbomo-Harmitt, Shahid Aziz, Adelaide De Vecchi, Andrew King, Oleg Aslanidi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Self-Supervised Learning, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 该研究提出一种预训练AI模型，结合对比学习和多任务监督头来预测心肌梗死后的心血管疾病进展。模型在有限数据情况下表现显著优于从头训练的模型，AUC从0.608提升至0.794，证明了临床结构化ECG建模在医学小数据场景下的有效性。

<h3 id="227">[227] Beyond Anthropomorphism: Exploring the Roles of Perceived Non-humanity and Structural Similarity in Deep Self-Disclosure Toward Generative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13574) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Satoru Shibuya
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, AI Ethics, User Modeling
- **Summary**: 该研究探讨了用户向生成式AI进行深度自我披露的心理因素，发现感知的非人类性和结构相似性都能促进自我披露行为。基于2,400名参与者的调查数据，研究表明信任行为可能涉及拟人化感知之外的因素，高感知组的披露可能性是基线组的11倍以上。

<h3 id="228">[228] HetScene: Heterogeneity-Aware Diffusion for Dense Indoor Scene Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13586](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13586) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zini Chen, Junming Huang, Rong Zhang, Jiamin Xu, Cheng Peng, Chi Wang, Weiwei Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, 3D Vision, Scene Understanding
- **Summary**: 该论文提出HetScene框架，一种异构感知的扩散模型，用于生成可控且物理合理的密集室内场景。通过将物体分解为主要物体和次要物体，采用结构布局生成和上下文布局生成的两阶段方法，解决了现有方法在密集物体布局和复杂空间依赖关系建模方面的不足。

<h3 id="229">[229] OpenAaaS: An Open Agent-as-a-Service Framework for Distributed Materials-Informatics Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13618](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13618) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peng Kang, Bixuan Li, Xiaoya Huang, Shuo Shi, Weiqiao Zhou, Zhen Li, Yu Liu, Lei Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Material Discovery
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Wolido/OpenAaaS)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出OpenAaaS框架，一种开源的分层分布式Agent即服务架构，用于材料智能设计研究。框架遵循"代码流动、数据不动"原则，使子代理能够保留对本地数据的完全控制权，同时实现跨机构的安全协作，并通过两个案例研究验证了其有效性。

<h3 id="230">[230] Causality-Aware End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Ego-Centric Joint Scene Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13646](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13646) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seokha Moon, Minseung Lee, Joon Seo, Jinkyu Kim, Jungbeom Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Causal Inference, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 该论文提出CaAD框架，一种因果感知的端到端自动驾驶方法，通过建模自车与周围智能体之间的因果依赖关系来解决现有方法忽略因果交互的问题。在Bench2Drive和NAVSIM基准上分别取得了87.53驾驶分数和91.1 PDMS的优异性能。

<h3 id="231">[231] NAACA: Training-Free NeuroAuditory Attentive Cognitive Architecture with Oscillatory Working Memory for Salience-Driven Attention Gating</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13651](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13651) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhongju Yuan, Geraint Wiggins, Dick Botteldooren
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio Processing, Attention Mechanism, Cognitive Science
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出NAACA，一种无需训练的神经听觉注意认知架构，通过振荡工作记忆实现显著性驱动的注意力门控。在XD-Violence数据集上，将AudioQwen的平均精度从53.50%提升至70.60%，同时减少了不必要的模型调用。

<h3 id="232">[232] Beyond Perplexity: A Geometric and Spectral Study of Low-Rank Pre-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13652](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13652) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Namrata Shivagunde, Vijeta Deshpande, Sherin Muckatira, Anna Rumshisky
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Model Compression, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 该研究从几何和光谱角度分析了五种低秩预训练方法，发现即使验证困惑度相近，低秩方法与全秩训练收敛到不同的解。研究引入16个指标评估损失景观、光谱结构和激活相似性，证明困惑度不是评估模型质量的唯一标准。

<h3 id="233">[233] Weakly Supervised Segmentation as Semantic-Based Regularization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13674) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stefano Colamonaco, Andrei-Bogdan Florea, Jaron Maene
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Weak Supervision, Neurosymbolic AI
- **Summary**: 该论文提出一种神经符号方法，将可微模糊逻辑与深度分割模型结合，利用弱标注和领域先验作为逻辑约束来微调SAM。实验表明该方法能生成更高质量的伪标签，在Pascal VOC和REFUGE2数据集上达到了最先进的分割精度。

<h3 id="234">[234] Cross Modality Image Translation In Medical Imaging Using Generative Frameworks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giulia Romoli, Alessia Capoccia, Filippo Ruffini, Francesco Di Feola, Luca Boldrini, Arturo Chiti, Renato Cuocolo, Tugba Akinci D&#39;Antonoli, Fatemeh Darvizeh, Marcello Di Pumpo, Bradley J. Erickson, Liu Fang, Deborah Fazzini, Paola Feraco, Fabrizia Gelardi, Francesco Gossetti, Ana Isabel Hernáiz Ferrer, Michail E. Klontzas, Seyedmehdi Payabvash, Katrine Riklund, Sara N. Strandberg, Valerio Guarrasi, Paolo Soda
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Synthesis, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 该研究对七种生成模型在医学图像跨模态翻译任务上进行了标准化比较评估，涵盖11个数据集和4种翻译方向。结果显示GAN方法在所有任务上优于潜在生成模型，视觉图灵测试表明17位医师对合成图像与真实图像的区分准确率仅为56.7%。

<h3 id="235">[235] A Hierarchical Language Model with Predictable Scaling Laws and Provable Benefits of Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13687) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jason Gaitonde, Frederic Koehler, Elchanan Mossel, Joonhyung Shin, Allan Sly
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Deep Learning Theory, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该研究引入一族具有层次结构的合成语言，用于精确分析上下文长度和推理在自回归生成中的作用。理论证明，具有对数级工作记忆的推理模型可以精确采样，相比需要线性上下文长度的普通自回归模型有指数级改进。

<h3 id="236">[236] The WidthWall: A Strict Expressivity Hierarchy for Hypergraph Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13690](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13690) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fengqing Jiang, Yuetai Li, Yichen Feng, Kaiyuan Zheng, Luyao Niu, Bhaskar Ramasubramanian, Basel Alomair, Linda Bushnell, Radha Poovendran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Deep Learning Theory, Expressivity
- **Summary**: 该研究证明了超图神经网络的表达能力由其能检测和计数的小模式决定，并提出了"宽度墙"概念——一个基本架构限制。研究框架统一刻画了15种HGNN架构，精确识别了团扩张丢失的信息，并提出了密度感知模型来扩展表达能力。

<h3 id="237">[237] RTLC -- Research, Teach-to-Learn, Critique: A three-stage prompting paradigm inspired by the Feynman Learning Technique that lifts LLM-as-judge accuracy on JudgeBench with no fine-tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13695](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13695) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrea Morandi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Prompt Engineering, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出RTLC三阶段提示范式，受费曼学习法启发，将单一黑盒LLM提升为思维集成判断器。在JudgeBench-GPT上，Claude 3.7 Sonnet的成对准确率从64.6%提升至78.6%，无需微调、检索或外部工具。

<h3 id="238">[238] Identifying AI Web Scrapers Using Canary Tokens</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13706](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13706) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Steven Seiden, Triss Ren, Caroline Zhang, Taein Kim, Enze Liu, Emily Wenger
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, LLM Security, Web Scraping
- **Summary**: 该论文提出一种自动推断LLM相关网络爬虫的技术，通过向每个访问的爬虫提供唯一的金丝雀令牌，然后提示LLM获取网站信息来识别爬虫来源。实验在22个生产LLM系统上验证了该方法的有效性，发现了多个未公开披露的爬虫-LLM关联。

<h3 id="239">[239] Children's English Reading Story Generation via Supervised Fine-Tuning of Compact LLMs with Controllable Difficulty and Safety</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13709](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13709) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qian Shen, Fanghua Cao, Min Yao, Shlok Gilda, Bonnie J. Dorr, Walter L. Leite
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Fine-Tuning, Text Generation
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 该研究通过监督微调8B参数的紧凑LLM来生成儿童英语阅读故事，使教育工作者能够针对阅读水平和错误模式进行控制。评估结果显示，经过适当微调的小型模型在难度相关指标上优于零样本GPT-4o和Llama 3.3 70B，且几乎没有安全问题。

<h3 id="240">[240] Humanwashing -- It Should Leave You Feeling Dirty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13723](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13723) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ben Wilson, Matimba Swana, Peter Winter, Matt Roach
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, AI Safety, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Venue**: HHAI 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文批判性地分析了"人在回路"这一隐喻在AI决策系统中的使用，指出这种说法类似于"漂绿"，可能掩盖了实际的过程和结果。作者认为需要更仔细地审视人工监督的实际含义，而不是简单地依赖这个隐喻来暗示安全性。

<h3 id="241">[241] AnyFlow: Any-Step Video Diffusion Model with On-Policy Flow Map Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchao Gu, Guian Fang, Yuxin Jiang, Weijia Mao, Song Han, Han Cai, Mike Zheng Shou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了AnyFlow，首个基于流映射的任意步数视频扩散蒸馏框架，通过优化完整的ODE采样轨迹而非固定步数蒸馏来解决一致性蒸馏在测试时扩展性差的问题。该方法引入流映射反向模拟实现高效在线策略蒸馏，在少步采样场景下达到或超越一致性方法性能，同时随采样步数增加而扩展。

<h3 id="242">[242] Coordinating Multiple Conditions for Trajectory-Controlled Human Motion Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13729](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13729) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deli Cai, Haoyang Ma, Changxing Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Motion, Diffusion Model, Motion Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文提出CMC框架，通过分治策略协调文本和轨迹条件进行轨迹控制的人体运动生成。该框架包含轨迹控制和运动完成两个级联阶段，并引入选择性修复机制缓解过拟合问题，在HumanML3D和KIT数据集上实现了最先进的控制精度和运动质量。

<h3 id="243">[243] Robust and Explainable Bicuspid Aortic Valve Diagnosis Using Stacked Ensembles on Echocardiography</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13730](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13730) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christos Chrysanthos Nikolaidis, Vasileios Sachpekidis, Nikolas Moustakidis, Theofilos Moustakidis, Pavlos S. Efraimidis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Video Understanding, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 本文开发了一个可解释AI模型，使用堆叠集成方法在超声心动图上区分二叶式主动脉瓣和三叶式主动脉瓣。该模型在90例患者研究上达到0.907的F1分数和0.877的召回率，并利用Grad-CAM和SHAP实现透明的病例级可审计性。

<h3 id="244">[244] KVServe: Service-Aware KV Cache Compression for Communication-Efficient Disaggregated LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13734) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zedong Liu, Xinyang Ma, Dejun Luo, Hairui Zhao, Bing Lu, Wenjing Huang, Yida Gu, Xingchen Liu, Zheng Wei, Jinyang Liu, Dingwen Tao, Guangming Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Serving, Model Compression
- **Venue**: SIGCOMM 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了KVServe，首个面向分离式LLM服务的服务感知自适应KV通信压缩框架。该框架将KV压缩统一为模块化策略空间，引入贝叶斯分析引擎和服务感知在线控制器，在PD分离服务中实现高达9.13倍的JCT加速，在KV分离服务中实现高达32.8倍的TTFT降低。

<h3 id="245">[245] Weakly-Supervised Spatiotemporal Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13746) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Urvi Gianchandani, Praveen Tirupattur, Mubarak Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文探索了一种弱监督视频异常检测方法，仅使用视频级标签进行训练，将视频片段表示为正负包并应用多实例排序损失。该方法能够检测时空区域的异常，在UCF Crime2Local数据集上进行了验证。

<h3 id="246">[246] High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13768](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13768) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="247">[247] Where Does Reasoning Break? Step-Level Hallucination Detection via Hidden-State Transport Geometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13772](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13772) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="248">[248] (How) Do Large Language Models Understand High-Level Message Sequence Charts?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13773) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Reza Mousavi
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Software Engineering, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 本文研究了大型语言模型对高级消息序列图(HMSC)形式语义的理解能力，测试了Gemini-3、GPT-5.4和Qwen-3.6在129个语义任务上的表现。结果显示LLM对HMSC形式语义的理解有限(约52%准确率)，在抽象、组合和轨迹任务上表现较差。

<h3 id="249">[249] MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13779](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13779) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了MinT，一个用于LoRA后训练和在线服务的托管基础设施系统，可扩展至数百万个LLM。该系统保持基础模型驻留，通过服务接口管理LoRA适配器版本，在4B密集模型上实现18.3倍的步骤减少，支持百万级可寻址目录。

<h3 id="250">[250] LMPath: Language-Mediated Priors and Path Generation for Aerial Exploration</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了LMPath，一个利用语言模型和视觉基础模型为无人机搜索任务生成语言介导探索先验的流程。该方法根据地理围栏和目标提示生成语义区域先验，用于优化无人机路径规划，在真实无人机飞行和仿真中均优于传统路径规划方法。

<h3 id="251">[251] Amplification to Synthesis: A Comparative Analysis of Cognitive Operations Before and After Generative AI</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文比较了2016年和2024年美国总统选举期间X平台上的行为和语言协调模式，分析了超过133,000条帖子。研究发现2024年语料库呈现出与生成式AI参与一致的特征：原创内容从59%上升到93%，词汇重叠度从0.99降至0.27。

<h3 id="252">[252] ENSEMBITS: an alphabet of protein conformational ensembles</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13789](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13789) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文介绍了Ensembits，首个蛋白质构象集成标记器，能够捕获蛋白质动力学中的相关运动和替代构象状态。该方法使用残差VQ-VAE和帧蒸馏目标进行训练，在RMSF预测上优于所有相关方法，并在EC、GO、结合位点预测上匹配或超越静态标记器。

<h3 id="253">[253] Di-BiLPS: Denoising induced Bidirectional Latent-PDE-Solver under Sparse Observations</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了Di-BiLPS，一个在极稀疏观测下处理正向和逆向PDE问题的统一神经框架。该框架结合变分自编码器、潜在扩散模块和对比学习，引入PDE信息去噪算法，在极稀疏输入(低至3%)下实现最先进性能，并支持零样本超分辨率。

<h3 id="254">[254] Improving Reproducibility in Evaluation through Multi-Level Annotator Modeling</h3>

<h3 id="255">[255] Neurosymbolic Auditing of Natural-Language Software Requirements</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13817) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了VERIMED，一个结合LLM和SMT求解器的神经符号流程，用于审计医疗设备软件需求。该方法将需求翻译为形式逻辑，通过随机变化检测歧义，通过求解器查询暴露不一致性，在血液透析安全需求数据集上将验证准确率从55.4%提高到98.5%。

<h3 id="256">[256] Negation Neglect: When models fail to learn negations in training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13829](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13829) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Fine-Tuning, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 本文发现了否定忽视现象：在标记声明为虚假的文档上微调LLM会使模型相信该声明为真。实验表明，在否定文档上微调后，平均相信率从2.5%上升到88.6%，该现象在所有测试模型中均存在，对AI安全具有重要影响。

<h3 id="257">[257] Topology-Preserving Neural Operator Learning via Hodge Decomposition</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于Hodge分解的神经算子学习方法，能够在学习几何动态的同时保持拓扑结构。该方法利用Hodge正交性将不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动态分离，在几何图上实现了更高的精度和效率。

<h3 id="258">[258] EVA-Bench: A New End-to-end Framework for Evaluating Voice Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13841](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13841) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="259">[259] WARDEN: Endangered Indigenous Language Transcription and Translation with 6 Hours of Training Data</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了DERL，一个用于自主发现最优奖励结构的双层可微进化强化学习框架。与黑盒方法不同，DERL通过内循环验证性能的策略梯度更新元优化器，在具身智能体和数学推理任务中达到了最先进性能。

- **Summary**: 该论文使用演化博弈论模型研究了AI能力不平等对网络安全的影响，发现通过补贴承诺防御者可以显著提高强防御采用率并改善系统整体韧性。

<h3 id="305">[305] SimWorld Studio: Automatic Environment Generation with Evolving Coding Agent for Embodied Agent Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09423](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09423) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoqiang Kang, Xiaokang Ye, Yuhan Liu, Siddhant Hitesh Mantri, Lingjun Mao, James Fleming, Drishti Regmi, Lianhui Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, LLM Agent, Simulation
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了SimWorld Studio，一个基于Unreal Engine 5的开源平台，用于自动生成演进的具身学习环境。其核心组件SimCoder是一个工具/技能增强的编码代理，能够编写和执行引擎级代码来构建物理基础的3D世界，并通过验证器反馈自我进化。实验表明，环境生成与具身学习的协同进化在导航任务上带来了显著的性能提升。

<h3 id="306">[306] EpiGraph: Building Generalists for Evidence-Intensive Epilepsy Reasoning in the Wild</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09505) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuyang Dai, Zheng Chen, Jathurshan Pradeepkumar, Yasuko Matsubara, Jimeng Sun, Yasushi Sakurai, Yushun Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Medical AI, RAG
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LabRAI/EEG-KG)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了EpiGraph，一个大规模癫痫知识图谱和基准测试，整合了48,166篇同行评审论文和七种临床资源，包含24,324个实体和32,009个证据支撑的三元组。基于此构建的EpiBench定义了五个临床任务，包括临床决策、EEG报告生成、药物基因组精准医学、治疗推荐和深度研究规划。实验表明，将EpiGraph与Graph-RAG结合可在所有任务上持续提升性能，其中药物基因组推理提升最大（+30-41%）。

<h3 id="307">[307] expo: Exploration-prioritized policy optimization via adaptive kl regulation and gaussian curriculum sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09923](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09923) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mingxiong Lin, Zhangquan Gong, Maowen Tang, Qian Li, Chuangchuang Wang, Jian Ma, Sutian Huang, Kai Tang, Haonan Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了探索优先策略优化（EXPO），解决了GRPO算法在LLM数学推理中存在的两个低效问题：固定KL惩罚系数过度限制探索，以及均匀采样忽略了难度适中的问题提供最有价值的梯度信号。EXPO引入了准确率条件KL缩放（AKL）和高斯课程采样（GCS）两个轻量级模块，在六个数学推理基准上取得了显著改进，AIME 2025 pass@32提升了13.34个百分点。

<h3 id="308">[308] IndustryBench: Probing the Industrial Knowledge Boundaries of LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10267](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10267) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Songlin Bai, Xintong Wang, Linlin Yu, Bin Chen, Zhiang Xu, Yuyang Sheng, Changtong Zan, Xiaofeng Zhu, Yizhe Zhang, Jiru Li, Mingze Guo, Ling Zou, Yalong Li, Chengfu Huo, Liang Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了IndustryBench，一个包含2,049项的工业采购QA基准测试，基于中国国家标准和结构化工业产品记录构建。该基准按七项能力维度、十个行业类别和难度层级组织，并提供了英语、俄语和越南语的翻译版本。评估显示，当前最佳系统在0-3评分标准下仅达到2.083分，扩展推理反而会降低安全调整后的分数，表明工业LLM评估需要源依据和感知安全的诊断方法。

<h3 id="309">[309] GESR: A Genetic Programming-Based Symbolic Regression Method with Gene Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10685) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanjie Li, Liping Zhang, Min Wu, Weijun Li, Lina Yu, Jingyi Liu, Yusong Deng, Mingzhu Wan, Xin Ning
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Symbolic Regression, Evolutionary Computation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了GESR，一种基于基因编辑的符号回归方法。传统遗传编程中的变异和交叉是完全随机的，而GESR训练了两个BERT模型作为"上帝之手"：一个利用BERT的掩码语言建模能力指导基因变异，另一个通过预测交叉点来指导个体基因的交叉。实验结果表明，GESR相比传统GP算法显著提高了计算效率，并在多个符号回归任务上取得了优异的整体性能。

<h3 id="310">[310] Selective Off-Policy Reference Tuning with Plan Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11505) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Duc Anh Le, Tien-Phat Nguyen, Thien Huu Nguyen, Linh Ngo Van, Trung Le
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SORT（选择性离策略参考调优与计划引导），解决了GRPO风格方法在所有采样轨迹都失败的困难提示上停滞的问题。SORT从参考解中推导计划，比较有计划和无计划条件下的token概率，对在计划条件下变得更可预测的token赋予更高权重。在三个骨干模型和八个推理基准上的实验表明，SORT在较弱模型上取得了最大的改进。

<h3 id="311">[311] Explaining and Breaking the Safety-Helpfulness Ceiling via Preference Dimensional Expansion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11679) (replaced)
- **Authors**: ShiYing Huang, Liang Lin, Yuer Li, Kaiwen Luo, Zhenhong Zhou, An Zhang, Junhao Dong, Kun Wang, Zhigang Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, RLHF
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Shiying-Huang/MORA-MPA)
- **Summary**: 本文从多维奖励的新视角解决了LLM多目标对齐中帮助性与无害性之间的零和冲突问题。通过分析不同奖励维度的模型输出，作者发现多目标冲突源于提示本身限制了可实现的多维奖励。基于此，提出了MORA（多目标奖励同化）方法，通过预采样隔离单奖励提示并重写问题以融入多维意图。实验表明，MORA在序列对齐中实现了5%-12.4%的单偏好改进，在同时多目标对齐中实现了4.6%的整体奖励提升。

<h3 id="312">[312] Multi-Modal World Model for Physical Robot Interactions: Simultaneous Visual and Tactile Predictions for Enhanced Accuracy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2304.11193](https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.11193) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Willow Mandil, Amir Ghalamzan-E
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: World Model, Robotics, Tactile Sensing
- **Venue**: Robotics and Autonomous Systems
- **Summary**: 本文研究了在物理机器人交互的预测感知系统中整合触觉和视觉信息。作者引入了两个使用磁性触觉传感器收集的新型机器人推动数据集，证明了触觉-视觉整合在物理模糊交互场景下能提高预测准确性和鲁棒性，而在视觉上可推断的场景中改进有限。结果表明，视触预测在物理模糊的交互场景中提供最大收益。

<h3 id="313">[313] BEAVER: An Enterprise Benchmark for Text-to-SQL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2409.02038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02038) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Peter Baile Chen, Devin Yang, Weiyue Li, Fabian Wenz, Yi Zhang, Nesime Tatbul, Michael Cafarella, Çağatay Demiralp, Michael Stonebraker
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了BEAVER，首个源自私有数据仓库的文本到SQL基准测试，包含来自真实查询日志的9,128个问题-SQL对和19个不同领域的812张表。该基准解决了企业环境中复杂模式、领域知识和复杂分析查询的挑战，并提供了五个关键子任务的人工标注和评估指标。评估显示，使用GPT-5.2的最先进代理框架仅达到10.8%的准确率，揭示了与企业基准相比的显著性能差距。

<h3 id="314">[314] Repurposing Image Diffusion Models for Training-Free Music Style Transfer on Mel-spectrograms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.15913](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15913) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Heehwan Wang, Joonwoo Kwon, Sooyoung Kim, Jungwoo Seo, Shinjae Yoo, Yuewei Lin, Jiook Cha
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Diffusion Model, Audio Processing
- **Venue**: ICIP 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Sooyyoungg/Stylus.git)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Stylus，一个无需训练的框架，将预训练的图像扩散模型重新用于Mel频谱图域的音乐风格迁移。通过将音频视为结构化的时频图像，Stylus在保留源结构查询的同时注入风格键值来操纵自注意力。该方法引入了相位保留重建策略和受无分类器引导启发的可控风格化控制。评估显示，Stylus在内容保留上比基线高34.1%，在感知质量上高25.7%。

<h3 id="315">[315] Visual Accommodation: Rethinking Image Scale as a Learnable Variable for Object Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.06341](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06341) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daeun Seo, Hoeseok Yang, Sihyeong Park, Hyungshin Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Test-Time Adaptation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Ciliary-DETR框架，通过引入轻量级尺度预测器在测试时动态调整输入分辨率，类似于生物视觉的调节机制。该方法通过参数化公式和损失驱动目标解决最优输入尺度不可观测的挑战，实现了灵活高效的单次推理，弥合了训练时鲁棒性与测试时适应性之间的差距。

<h3 id="316">[316] AIvaluateXR: An Evaluation Framework for on-Device AI in XR with Benchmarking Results</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.15761](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15761) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dawar Khan, Xinyu Liu, Omar Mena, Donggang Jia, Alexandre Kouyoumdjian, Ivan Viola
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Test-Time Adaptation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Ciliary-DETR（原名Elastic-DETR），一个类似于生物调节机制的测试时分辨率调整框架。该方法引入了一个轻量级尺度预测器，可以在广泛的输入尺度范围内动态估计测试时的尺度因子。通过参数化期望的缩放行为并使用损失驱动目标来指导尺度优化，该方法实现了灵活高效的单次推理，弥合了训练时鲁棒性和测试时适应性之间的差距。

<h3 id="317">[317] DeePen: Penetration Testing for Audio Deepfake Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.20427](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20427) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nicolas Müller, Piotr Kawa, Adriana Stan, Thien-Phuc Doan, Souhwan Jung, Wei Herng Choong, Philip Sperl, Konstantin Böttinger
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Adversarial Robustness, Penetration Testing
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了DeePen，一种用于评估深度伪造检测分类器鲁棒性的系统渗透测试方法。该方法无需事先了解或访问目标深度伪造检测模型，而是利用精心选择的信号处理修改（称为攻击）来评估模型漏洞。分析表明，所有测试的系统都存在弱点，可以通过简单的时间拉伸或回声添加等操作可靠欺骗，某些攻击即使在重新训练后仍然持续有效。

<h3 id="318">[318] VIPO: Value Function Inconsistency Penalized Offline Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.11944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11944) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuyang Chen, Keyu Yan, Guojian Wang, Lin Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Offline RL, Model-Based RL
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了VIPO，一种新颖的基于模型的离线强化学习算法，通过引入价值估计的自监督反馈来增强模型训练。该方法通过额外最小化直接从离线数据学习的价值与从模型估计的价值之间的不一致性来学习模型。综合评估表明，VIPO能够高效学习高精度模型，在D4RL和NeoRL基准的几乎所有任务上都达到了最先进的性能。

<h3 id="319">[319] Systematic Failures in Collective Reasoning under Distributed Information in Multi-Agent LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.11556](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11556) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Li, Aoi Naito, Hirokazu Shirado
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文引入了HiddenBench，一个基于隐藏档案范式的65任务基准测试，用于评估多代理LLM在分布式信息下的集体推理能力。对15个前沿LLM的评估显示，多代理LLM在分布式信息下仅达到30.1%的准确率，而拥有完整信息的单代理达到80.7%。研究发现代理无法识别或处理潜在信息不对称，导致过早收敛于共享证据而忽略关键的分布式事实，并提出了一种轻量级结构化通信协议来改善集体推理。

<h3 id="320">[320] Table-R1: Region-based Reinforcement Learning for Table Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.12415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12415) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenhe Wu, Jian Yang, Zhongjiang He, Changzai Pan, Jie Zhang, Jiaheng Liu, Xianjie Wu, Yu Zhao, Shuangyong Song, Yongxiang Li, Zhoujun Li, Xueling Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Table Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了Table-R1，一种通过将区域证据整合到推理步骤中来增强LLM表格理解的强化学习方法。该方法采用区域增强监督微调（RE-SFT）和表格感知群组相对策略优化（TARPO），后者引入混合奖励系统来动态平衡区域准确性和答案正确性。实验表明，Table-R1在三个基准数据集上实现了平均14.36分的性能提升，甚至超越了参数量十倍的基线模型，同时将响应token消耗减少了67.5%。

<h3 id="321">[321] A3 : an Analytical Low-Rank Approximation Framework for Attention</h3>

<h3 id="322">[322] Efficient compression of neural networks and datasets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.17469](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17469) (replaced)

<h3 id="323">[323] NFR: Neural Feature-Guided Non-Rigid Shape Registration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.22445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22445) (replaced)

<h3 id="325">[325] T-TExTS (Teaching Text Expansion for Teacher Scaffolding): Enhancing Text Selection in High School Literature through Knowledge Graph-Based Recommendation</h3>

<h3 id="327">[327] LoRA-Mixer: Coordinate Modular LoRA Experts Through Serial Attention Routing</h3>

<h3 id="328">[328] Robotic Manipulation by Imitating Generated Videos Without Physical Demonstrations</h3>

<h3 id="329">[329] Where Do Reasoning Models Refuse?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.03167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03167) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Security, LLM Alignment

- **Summary**: 本文研究了推理模型中拒绝决策在思维链中的发生位置，发现在蒸馏模型中思维链开头的细微差异可以完全决定拒绝结果，并从模型激活中提取了线性拒绝方向。

<h3 id="330">[330] RDMA: Cost Effective Agent-Driven Rare Disease Mining from Electronic Health Records</h3>

<h3 id="337">[337] Unmasking Puppeteers: Leveraging Biometric Leakage to Expose Impersonation in AI-Based Videoconferencing</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种检索增强的多智能体框架，用于自动生成医疗对话系统的实例级评估标准。该方法通过将检索到的医学内容分解为原子事实，并结合用户交互约束来合成可验证的细粒度评估标准，在HealthBench和LLMEval-Med数据集上显著优于GPT-4o基线，并展现出良好的跨语言泛化能力。

<h3 id="361">[361] High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication I</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.17187](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17187) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Or Ordentlich, Yury Polyanskiy
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出一种检索增强的多智能体框架，用于自动生成医学对话系统的实例特定评估标准。该方法将评估建立在权威医学证据之上，在临床意图对齐方面显著优于GPT-4o基线。

<h3 id="362">[362] GUIGuard-Bench: Toward a General Evaluation for Privacy-Preserving GUI Agents</h3>

<h3 id="363">[363] SynCABEL: Synthetic Contextualized Augmentation for Biomedical Entity Linking</h3>

<h3 id="364">[364] Improving Classifier-Free Guidance of Flow Matching via Manifold Projection</h3>

<h3 id="365">[365] Optimization, Generalization and Differential Privacy Bounds for Gradient Descent on Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks</h3>

<h3 id="366">[366] RAG-GNN: Integrating Retrieved Knowledge with Graph Neural Networks for Precision Medicine</h3>

<h3 id="367">[367] Preserve-Then-Quantize: Balancing Rank Budgets for Quantization Error Reconstruction in LLMs</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Model Compression, LLM Inference

- **Summary**: 本文提出结构化残差重建（SRR），一种LLM量化的秩分配框架，在量化前保留顶部奇异子空间，使用剩余秩进行误差重建。该方法在PTQ中持续降低困惑度，并在2位QPEFT上获得5.9个百分点的GLUE提升。

<h3 id="368">[368] Auditing Sybil: Explaining Deep Lung Cancer Risk Prediction Through Generative Interventional Attributions</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Explainable AI, Interpretability
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出S(H)NAP，一种模型无关的审计框架，通过生成式干预归因解释深度肺癌风险预测模型Sybil。使用3D扩散桥建模系统修改解剖特征，揭示了模型对临床无关伪影的敏感性等关键失效模式。

<h3 id="369">[369] Aligning Forest and Trees in Images & Long Captions for Visually Grounded Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02977](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02977) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Byeongju Woo, Zilin Wang, Byeonghyun Pak, Sangwoo Mo, Stella X. Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出CAFT，一种层次化视觉语言模型，通过在中间表示层学习局部文本-区域对齐、在最终表示层学习全局图像-文本对齐，解决了现有模型难以理解长篇详细描述的问题。该方法无需显式区域级监督即可学习细粒度表示，在六个长文本检索基准上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="370">[370] Exponential Approximation Rates and Parameter Efficiency of Learnable Bernstein Activations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.04264](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04264) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ibrahim Albool, Malak Gamal El-Din, Salma Elmalaki, Yasser Shoukry
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.NA
- **Tags**: Activation Function, Deep Learning Theory, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 该论文对使用可学习Bernstein多项式激活函数的深度网络(DBNs)进行理论分析，证明其逼近误差以O(n^{-L})的速率衰减，比ReLU架构快指数级。实验表明DBNs在大多数架构上实现超过70%的参数减少，训练轮次减少至26%即可达到相同损失，最终损失降低达45%。

<h3 id="371">[371] Entropy Aware Reward Guidance for Diffusion Language Model Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05000) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Atula Tejaswi, Litu Rout, Constantine Caramanis, Sanjay Shakkottai, Sujay Sanghavi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Diffusion Model, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出EntRGi方法，解决离散扩散语言模型的奖励引导问题。该方法利用扩散模型的预测熵，在逐token基础上动态插值连续token松弛和采样硬token，在测试时适应和后训练两种设置下均优于现有方法。

<h3 id="372">[372] interwhen: A Generalizable Framework for Steering Reasoning Models with Test-time Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11202](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11202) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vishak K Bhat, Prateek Chanda, Vijval Ekbote, Ashmit Khandelwal, Maitreyi Swaroop, Vineeth N. Balasubramanian, Subbarao Kambhampati, Nagarajan Natarajan, Amit Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Test-Time Adaptation, Self-Verification
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/microsoft/interwhen)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出interwhen框架，一种单轨迹验证方法，通过在推理过程中对中间推理轨迹提供反馈来引导模型行为。该方法支持从自然语言策略文档自动合成验证器，在推理基准和智能体基准上显著提升任务完成率和策略合规性。

<h3 id="373">[373] Test-Time Training with KV Binding Is Secretly Linear Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.21204](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21204) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junchen Liu, Sven Elflein, Or Litany, Zan Gojcic, Ruilong Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Test-Time Adaptation, KV Cache, Linear Attention
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文挑战了测试时训练(TTT)与KV绑定的传统记忆化解释，证明了一类广泛的TTT架构可以表示为学习型线性注意力算子。这一新视角不仅解释了之前令人困惑的模型行为，还实现了架构简化与并行化，将多种TTT变体系统地归约为标准线性注意力形式。

<h3 id="374">[374] Zatom-1: Towards a Multimodal Foundation Model for 3D Molecules and Materials</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.22251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22251) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alex Morehead, Miruna Cretu, Antonia Panescu, Rishabh Anand, Maurice Weiler, Tynan Perez, Samuel Blau, Steven Farrell, Wahid Bhimji, Anubhav Jain, Hrushikesh Sahasrabuddhe, Pietro Lio, Tommi Jaakkola, Rafael Gomez-Bombarelli, Rex Ying, N. Benjamin Erichson, Michael W. Mahoney
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Linear Attention, Test-Time Adaptation, Memory Architecture
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Zatom-AI/zatom)
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示了带有KV绑定的测试时训练(TTT)可被表达为一种学习型线性注意力算子，而非传统的记忆化解释。这一视角使架构简化、支持完全并行化公式，并将多种TTT变体系统性地归约为标准线性注意力形式。

<h3 id="375">[375] Decentralized Ranking Aggregation via Gossip: Convergence and Robustness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.22847](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22847) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kerrian Le Caillec, Anna Van Elst, Igor Colin, Stephan Clémençon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML

- **Summary**: 该论文研究去中心化环境下的排序聚合问题，提出基于随机gossip通信的方法，使自治智能体能够仅通过局部交互计算全局排序共识。该方法在收敛性、对污染节点的鲁棒性和可扩展性方面提供理论保证。

<h3 id="376">[376] Kiwi-Edit: Versatile Video Editing via Instruction and Reference Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02175](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02175) (replaced)
